B. Transfers an adenyl group from ATP to ribose-5-phosphate - This would produce an AMP-linked intermediate, not what PRPP synthetase does.

C. Transfers a pyrophosphoryl group from ATP to ribose-5-phosphate, producing PRPP and AMP - This is exactly the reaction catalyzed by PRPP synthetase (ribose-phosphate diphosphokinase). ATP + R5P → PRPP + AMP.

D. Oxidizes ribose-5-phosphate to ribonolactone while reducing NAD⁺ - This is an oxidoreductase reaction, not what PRPP synthetase does.

The answer is C.
